\"People are going to get hurt because of you,\" even when every channel is muted and no one else hears anything.\n- key: S024\n  name: Depressed_Mood\n  description: A state where a sad, empty, or hopeless mood persists.\n  manifestation: Most days I feel like this heavy gray mood settles over me before I even get out of bed, and I can tear up in my car before shift over nothing more than seeing the casino lights.\n- key: S025\n  name: Loss_of_Interest\n  description: A state where interest or pleasure in previously enjoyed activities is markedly diminished.\n  manifestation: I used to look forward to salsa class, softball, building PCs, and having people over for board games, but lately I just ignore the texts and sit at home like none of it can reach me.\n- key: S028\n  name: Like, saying I missed something, or that people are going to get hurt because of me. It comes through the speakers, even when they’re muted.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever hear the voices when you're not at work?\n\nPatient: No… not really. Not in my head, and not like someone standing in the room. Just during the quiet stretches, even when the channels are muted.\n\nDoctor: Do the voices ever say things that you think or believe yourself?\n\nPatient: Sometimes it’s like… it says what I’m already afraid of. Like that I missed something, or that someone’s going to get hurt because of me. Like, “You missed it, Ruth,” or “People are going to get hurt because of you.” I wouldn’t want to believe that, but it gets in my head.\n\nDoctor: Can you make the voices stop when you want them to?\n\nPatient: No. I mean… I try to ignore them, or turn things down, but they’ll still come through during the quiet parts. Even muted, sometimes. I can’t really make it stop.\n\nDoctor: Can you make the voices say something specific when you want them to?\n\nPatient: No… I can’t make them say what I want. It’s more like they just come through, saying those same kinds of things… that I missed something, or someone’s going to get hurt because of me. I don’t control it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the voices you hear through the monitor speakers are real?\n\nPatient: I don’t know. In the moment it feels real… like it’s coming through the speakers. But then everything’s muted, and no one else hears it, so… I know that doesn’t make sense. It’s more like they just come through, saying those same kinds of things… that I missed something, or someone’s going to get hurt because of me. I don’t control it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the voices you hear through the monitor speakers are real?\n\nPatient: I don’t know. In the moment it feels real… like it’s coming through the speakers. But then everything’s muted, and no one else hears it, so… I know that doesn’t make sense. That’s what scared me enough to come in.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like you're thinking something and it's not really you thinking it?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure. I don’t think so… I mean, my thoughts feel like mine, even when they’re not good. I haven’t really noticed it like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had an episode like this before where you heard voices or had similar experiences?\n\nPatient: No… not like this. I mean, I’ve had bad stretches before, but hearing voices through the monitors when everything’s muted… saying I missed something, or that people were going to get hurt because of me… that’s new. That’s why I came in.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other experiences where you saw or heard things that weren't there before this started happening at work?\n\nPatient: No… not before this. It’s really just been at work, in the surveillance room, during the quiet parts. I’ll hear it through the monitor speakers, even when everything’s muted… saying things like I missed something, or people are going to get hurt because of me. No one else hears it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period in your life where you had a break from reality that lasted for more than a day?\n\nPatient: No… not before this. I’ve felt low before, but I didn’t have anything like hearing accusations through the equipment or being so sure I was a danger to people. That’s all been tied to this same bad stretch, I think.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like the voices you hear are a part of your own mind or something outside of yourself?\n\nPatient: It feels… outside of me. Like it’s coming through the monitor speakers, not just a thought in my head. Even when everything’s muted. That’s what scares me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other times in your life when you heard voices or saw things that weren't there, outside of this experience at work?\n\nPatient: No… not really. It’s just been this thing at work, in the surveillance room. During the quiet stretches I hear voices through the monitor speakers, even when they’re muted. They say I missed something, or that people are going to get hurt because of me. I haven’t had that outside of this.\n\nDoctor: Have the voices through the monitor speakers affected your ability to do your job or interact with others?\n\nPatient: Yeah… it has. I’ve messed up some shift paperwork, which isn’t like me. And I’ve been keeping more to myself. I don’t really see friends much lately… I just don’t have it in me.\n\nAt home I’m mostly just doing the basics. Not cooking much, not having people over, not getting to projects. Just… getting through.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. What brings you in to see me today?\n\nPatient: I’m here because… something’s been happening at work that scared me.\n\nI work in surveillance, and during the quiet stretches I hear voices through the monitor speakers. They’ll say things like, “You missed it, Ruth,” or that people are going to get hurt because of me. But the channels are muted, and no one else seems to hear it.\n\nDoctor: Do the voices ever tell you to do something?\n\nPatient: Not exactly telling me to do something. It’s more… accusing me. Like, saying I missed something, or that people are going to get hurt because of me. It comes through the speakers, even when they’re muted.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever hear the voices when you're not at work?\n\nPatient: No… not really. It’s been in the surveillance room, during the quiet parts. Through the monitor speakers, even when everything’s muted. I haven’t noticed it happening outside of work.\n\nDoctor: Do you hear the voices only through the monitor speakers, or do you also hear them in your mind or in the environment around you?\n\nPatient: It’s… only through the monitor speakers, as far as I can tell. Not in my head, and not like someone standing in the room. Just during the quiet stretches, even when the channels are muted.\n\nDoctor: Do the voices ever say things that you think or believe yourself?\n\nPatient: Sometimes it’s like… it says what I’m already afraid of. Like that I missed something, or that someone’s going to get hurt because of me. I don’t know if I believe it exactly, but it feels hard to shake.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the voices are saying things that you would never think or believe yourself?\n\nPatient: I don’t think they’re completely foreign, no. It’s more like… the worst version of what I’m already scared of. Like, “You missed it, Ruth,” or “People are going to get hurt because of you.” I wouldn’t want to believe that, but it gets in my head.\n\nDoctor: Can you make the voices stop when you want them to?\n\nPatient: No. I mean… I try to ignore them, or turn things down, but they’ll still come through during the quiet parts. Even muted, sometimes. I can’t really make it stop.\n\nDoctor: Can you make the voices say something specific when you want them to?\n\nPatient: No… I can’t make them say what I want. It’s more like they just come through, saying those same kinds of things… that I missed something, or someone’s going to get hurt because of me. I don’t control it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the voices you hear through the monitor speakers are real?\n\nPatient: I don’t know. In the moment it feels real… like it’s coming through the speakers. But then everything’s muted, and no one else hears it, so… I know that doesn’t make sense. That’s what scared me enough to come in.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like you're thinking something and it's not really you thinking it?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure. I don’t think so… I mean, my thoughts feel like mine, even when they’re not good. I haven’t really noticed it like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had an episode like this before where you heard voices or had similar experiences?\n\nPatient: No… not like this. I mean, I’ve had bad stretches before, but hearing voices through the monitors when everything’s muted… saying I missed something, or that people were going to get hurt because of me… that’s new. That’s why I came in.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other experiences where you saw or heard things that weren't there before this started happening at work?\n\nPatient: No… not before this. It’s really just been at work, in the surveillance room, during the quiet parts. I’ll hear it through the monitor speakers, even when everything’s muted… saying things like I missed something, or people are going to get hurt because of me. No one else hears it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period in your life where you had a break from reality that lasted for more than a day?\n\nPatient: No… not before this. I’ve felt low before, but I didn’t have anything like hearing accusations through the equipment or being so sure I was a danger to people. That’s all been tied to this same bad stretch, I think.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like the voices you hear are a part of your own mind or something outside of yourself?\n\nPatient: It feels… outside of me. Like it’s coming through the monitor speakers, not just a thought in my head. Even when everything’s muted. That’s what scares me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other times in your life when you heard voices or saw things that weren't there, outside of this experience at work?\n\nPatient: No… not really. It’s just been this thing at work, in the surveillance room. During the quiet stretches I hear voices through the monitor speakers, even when they’re muted. They say I missed something, or that people are going to get hurt because of me. I haven’t had that outside of this.\n\nDoctor: Have the voices through the monitor speakers affected your ability to do your job or interact with others?\n\nPatient: Yeah… it has. I’ve messed up some shift paperwork, which isn’t like me. And I’ve been keeping more to myself. I don’t really see friends much lately… I just don’t have it in me.\n\nAt home I’m mostly just doing the basics. Not cooking much, not having people over, not getting to projects. Just… getting through.\n\nDoctor: Do you find it hard to express your thoughts or get your point across to others lately?\n\nPatient: Yeah… a little. At work people have asked me to repeat myself.\n\nAnd I’ll just… sit there staring at a report or the camera grid longer than I should before I can make myself move.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like the voices you heard through the monitor speakers were a sign of something being seriously wrong with your mind?\n\nPatient: Yeah… that’s kind of what scared me enough to come in.\n\nI kept thinking, if every channel is muted and I’m still hearing it… and no one else hears anything… then maybe something is really wrong.
